# Source-reference index

[Guide index](README.md) · [Complete contents](table-of-contents.md)

This index maps directly cited local source, interface, build, test, and upstream documentation files to the guides that cite them. It does not imply whole-file review; see each subsystem's coverage table. Directory links are excluded.

Regenerate with `python3 docs-ai/tools/build_source_index.py`.
